Kết quả chấm bài

Substantiated behaviors: 1. dl_lab.exe execution from explorer.exe (E02). 2. File write to C:\Users\admin\AppData\Roaming\SysHelper\syshelper.exe (E03). 3. Scheduled task creation (E04). 4. SHA256 hash 5D0E7A13C6B94F28E1A07D3B92C5F6084E1D7B3A60C9F25E8D41B7A3C02F9E64. 5. Timeline of 15:35:27–15:37:13. Defects: 1. Missing hash evidence in Bảng 7. 2. Contradictory timeline (15:35 vs 12:29). 3. Missing network activity evidence. 4. Placeholder in network mode. 5. Missing privilege evidence. 6. Unresolved evidence reference (E01). 7. Material false claim about MsMpEng.exe. 8. Inconsistent PID (9685 vs 2726). 9. Missing file path evidence. 10. Missing registry key evidence.
